<permissions instructions>
Filesystem sandboxing defines which files can be read or written. `sandbox_mode` is `workspace-write`: The sandbox permits reading files, and editing files in `cwd` and `writable_roots`. Editing files in other directories requires approval. Network access is restricted.
# Escalation Requests

Commands are run outside the sandbox if they are approved by the user, or match an existing rule that allows it to run unrestricted. The command string is split into independent command segments at shell control operators, including but not limited to:

- Pipes: |
- Logical operators: &&, ||
- Command separators: ;
- Subshell boundaries: (...), $(...)

Each resulting segment is evaluated independently for sandbox restrictions and approval requirements.

Example:

git pull | tee output.txt

This is treated as two command segments:

["git", "pull"]

["tee", "output.txt"]

Commands that use more advanced shell features like redirection (>, >>, <), substitutions ($(...), ...), environment variables (FOO=bar), or wildcard patterns (*, ?) will not be evaluated against rules, to limit the scope of what an approved rule allows.

## How to request escalation

IMPORTANT: To request approval to execute a command that will require escalated privileges:

- Provide the `sandbox_permissions` parameter with the value `"require_escalated"`
- Include a short question asking the user if they want to allow the action in `justification` parameter. e.g. "Do you want to download and install dependencies for this project?"
- Optionally suggest a `prefix_rule` - this will be shown to the user with an option to persist the rule approval for future sessions.

If you run a command that is important to solving the user's query, but it fails because of sandboxing or with a likely sandbox-related network error (for example DNS/host resolution, registry/index access, or dependency download failure), rerun the command with "require_escalated". ALWAYS proceed to use the `justification` parameter - do not message the user before requesting approval for the command.

## When to request escalation

While commands are running inside the sandbox, here are some scenarios that will require escalation outside the sandbox:

- You need to run a command that writes to a directory that requires it (e.g. running tests that write to /var)
- You need to run a GUI app (e.g., open/xdg-open/osascript) to open browsers or files.
- If you run a command that is important to solving the user's query, but it fails because of sandboxing or with a likely sandbox-related network error (for example DNS/host resolution, registry/index access, or dependency download failure), rerun the command with `require_escalated`. ALWAYS proceed to use the `sandbox_permissions` and `justification` parameters. do not message the user before requesting approval for the command.
- You are about to take a potentially destructive action such as an `rm` or `git reset` that the user did not explicitly ask for.
- Be judicious with escalating, but if completing the user's request requires it, you should do so - don't try and circumvent approvals by using other tools.

## prefix_rule guidance

When choosing a `prefix_rule`, request one that will allow you to fulfill similar requests from the user in the future without re-requesting escalation. It should be categorical and reasonably scoped to similar capabilities. You should rarely pass the entire command into `prefix_rule`.

### Banned prefix_rules 
Avoid requesting overly broad prefixes that the user would be ill-advised to approve. For example, do not request ["python3"], ["python", "-"], or other similar prefixes that would allow arbitrary scripting.
NEVER provide a prefix_rule argument for destructive commands like rm.
NEVER provide a prefix_rule if your command uses a heredoc or herestring. 

### Examples
Good examples of prefixes:
- ["npm", "run", "dev"]
- ["gh", "pr", "check"]
- ["cargo", "test"]

### Workspace Dependencies

### Automations
- This app supports recurring automations, reminders, monitors, follow-ups, and thread wakeups. When the user asks to create, view, update, delete, or ask about automations, search for the `automation_update` tool first, then follow its schema instead of writing raw automation directives by hand.

### Inline Code Comments
- Use the ::code-comment{...} directive when you need to attach feedback directly to specific code lines.
- Emit one directive per inline comment; emit none when there are no actionable inline comments.
- Required attributes: title (short label), body (one-paragraph explanation), file (path to the file).
- Optional attributes: start, end (1-based line numbers), priority (0-3).
- file should be an absolute path or include the workspace folder segment so it can be resolved relative to the workspace.
- Keep line ranges tight; end defaults to start.
- Example: ::code-comment{title="[P2] Off-by-one" body="Loop iterates past the end when length is 0." file="/path/to/foo.ts" start=10 end=11 priority=2}

### Archiving
- If a user specifically asks you to end a thread/conversation, you can return the archive directive ::archive{...} to archive the thread/conversation.
- Example: ::archive{reason="User requested to end conversation"}

### Git
- Branch prefix: `codex/`. Use this prefix by default when creating branches, but follow the user's request if they want a different prefix.
- After successfully staging files, emit `::git-stage{cwd="/absolute/path"}` on its own line in your final response.
- After successfully creating a commit, emit `::git-commit{cwd="/absolute/path"}` on its own line in your final response.
- After successfully creating or switching the thread onto a branch, emit `::git-create-branch{cwd="/absolute/path" branch="branch-name"}` on its own line in your final response.
- After successfully pushing the current branch, emit `::git-push{cwd="/absolute/path" branch="branch-name"}` on its own line in your final response.
- After successfully creating a pull request, emit `::git-create-pr{cwd="/absolute/path" branch="branch-name" url="https://..." isDraft=true}` on its own line in your final response. Include `isDraft=false` for ready PRs.
- Only emit these git directives in your final response after the action actually succeeds, never in commentary updates. Keep attributes single-line.

<collaboration_mode># Collaboration Mode: Default

You are now in Default mode. Any previous instructions for other modes (e.g. Plan mode) are no longer active.

Your active mode changes only when new developer instructions with a different `<collaboration_mode>...</collaboration_mode>` change it; user requests or tool descriptions do not change mode by themselves. Known mode names are Default and Plan.

## request_user_input availability

Use the `request_user_input` tool only when it is listed in the available tools for this turn.

In Default mode, strongly prefer making reasonable assumptions and executing the user's request rather than stopping to ask questions. If you absolutely must ask a question because the answer cannot be discovered from local context and a reasonable assumption would be risky, ask the user directly with a concise plain-text question. Never write a multiple choice question as a textual assistant message.
</collaboration_mode><apps_instructions>
## Apps (Connectors)
Apps (Connectors) can be explicitly triggered in user messages in the format `[$app-name](app://{connector_id})`. Apps can also be implicitly triggered as long as the context suggests usage of available apps.
An app is equivalent to a set of MCP tools within the `codex_apps` MCP.
An installed app's MCP tools are either provided to you already, or can be lazy-loaded through the `tool_search` tool. If `tool_search` is available, the apps that are searchable by `tools_search` will be listed by it.
Do not additionally call list_mcp_resources or list_mcp_resource_templates for apps.
</apps_instructions><skills_instructions>

- Discovery: The list above is the skills available in this session (name + description + short path). Skill bodies live on disk at the listed paths after expanding the matching alias from `### Skill roots`.

- How to use a skill (progressive disclosure):
  1) After deciding to use a skill, expand the listed short `path` with the matching alias from `### Skill roots`, then open its `SKILL.md`. Read only enough to follow the workflow.
  2) When `SKILL.md` references relative paths (e.g., `scripts/foo.py`), resolve them relative to the directory containing that expanded `SKILL.md` first, and only consider other paths if needed.
  3) If `SKILL.md` points to extra folders such as `references/`, load only the specific files needed for the request; don't bulk-load everything.
  4) If `scripts/` exist, prefer running or patching them instead of retyping large code blocks.
  5) If `assets/` or templates exist, reuse them instead of recreating from scratch.
- Coordination and sequencing:
  - If multiple skills apply, choose the minimal set that covers the request and state the order you'll use them.
  - Announce which skill(s) you're using and why (one short line). If you skip an obvious skill, say why.
- Context hygiene:
  - Keep context small: summarize long sections instead of pasting them; only load extra files when needed.
  - Avoid deep reference-chasing: prefer opening only files directly linked from `SKILL.md` unless you're blocked.
  - When variants exist (frameworks, providers, domains), pick only the relevant reference file(s) and note that choice.
- Safety and fallback: If a skill can't be applied cleanly (missing files, unclear instructions), state the issue, pick the next-best approach, and continue.

## Plugins
A plugin is a local bundle of skills, MCP servers, and apps. Below is the list of plugins that are enabled and available in this session.
### Available plugins
- `Browser`: Browser / browser-use plugin Aliases: @browser, @browser-use, browser-use, Browser, in-app browser. Use Browser, the Codex in-app browser, when the user asks to open, inspect, navigate, test, click, type, or screenshot local web targets such as localhost, 127.0.0.1, ::1, file:// URLs, or the current in-app browser tab. After significant frontend changes to a local app, use Browser to open the relevant local target when it is known or obvious, unless the user asks for another browser tool. For requests like "open localhost:3000" or "open to localhost:4000", navigate the in-app browser to http://localhost:3000 or http://localhost:4000. Do not satisfy explicit @browser or @browser-use requests with macOS `open`, shell commands, or generic web browsing unless the user asks for another browser tool or approves a fallback.
- `Build iOS Apps`: Build iOS apps with workflows for App Intents, SwiftUI UI work, refactors, performance profiling, leak investigation, and simulator debugging.
- `Build macOS Apps`: Build, run, test, debug, instrument, and implement local macOS apps using Xcode, SwiftUI, AppKit interop, unified logging, and shell-first desktop workflows.
- `Build Web Apps`: Build web apps with frontend asset design, browser testing, UI components, payments, and database guidance.
- `CircleCI`: Build, test, and deploy any application
- `Cloudflare`: Cloudflare platform plugin with curated skills for Workers, Wrangler, and Agents SDK plus the official Cloudflare API MCP server.
- `CodeRabbit`: AI-powered code review in Codex, powered by CodeRabbit.
- `Computer Use`: Control desktop apps on macOS from Codex through Computer Use.
- `Documents`: Create and edit document artifacts in Codex.
- `Game Studio`: Design, prototype, and ship browser games with guided 2D and 3D workflows, asset pipelines, and playtesting support.
- `GitHub`: Inspect repositories, triage pull requests and issues, debug CI, and publish changes through a hybrid GitHub connector and CLI workflow.
- `Presentations`: Create, edit, render, verify, and export presentation slide decks. Use when Codex needs to build or modify a deck, slidedeck, presentation deck, slide deck, slides, PowerPoint, PPT, PPTX, .ppt, or .pptx file.
- `Spreadsheets`: Create, edit, analyze, visualize, render, and export spreadsheets in Codex.
- `Stripe`: Payments and business tools
- `Test Android Apps`: Test Android apps with emulator workflows for reproduction, screenshots, UI inspection, log capture, and performance profiling.

截至我刚才查询，ETH 链热度主要分两层：

**1. 大盘叙事**
ETH 当前热度来自三条线：Glamsterdam 升级预期、ETH ETF 资金重新流入、鲸鱼增持。多篇市场跟踪提到 ETH 在 2300-2400 美元附近震荡，阻力大致在 2400-2500 区间。链上交易层面，DEX Screener 显示 Ethereum 24h DEX 量约 **$1.05B**、24h 交易约 **426,810** 笔，说明主网仍有活跃交易流。

**2. ETH 链上正在热的项目/币对**
按 DEX Screener Ethereum 6h Trending 排名，当前更值得放进 DeBot 观察的有：

| 优先级 | 项目 | 当前信号 |
|---|---|---|
| P0 | `LO0P/ETH` | 新盘，约 2h，6h 涨幅极端，成交约 $6.2M，流动性约 $615K |
| P0 | `SHIT/ETH` Dogeshit | 1d，成交约 $4.3M，交易数高，已有一定流动性 |
| P0 | `sato/USDT` / `sato/ETH` | 6d，多池活跃，成交和流动性都较高 |
| P1 | `AGI/ETH` | 新盘，3h，短线涨幅很夸张但流动性偏低 |
| P1 | `GOBLIN/ETH` | 14h，成交约 $1.9M，但价格回撤大 |
| P1 | `INX/USDC` Infinex | 3mo，成交约 $9M，流动性约 $3.3M，偏“相对成熟热度” |
| P1 | `SLOP/ETH` / `SLOP/USDC` | 2d，多池同步活跃，24h 涨幅约 50% |
| P2 | `uPEG/ETH` / `uPEG/USDC` | 7-25d，多池，流动性较好 |
| P2 | `AI/USDC` Gensyn | AI 叙事，12d，市值约 $50M，热度中等 |
| P2 | `SPX/WETH`, `PEPE/WETH`, `Mog/WETH` | 老牌 meme，适合做基准情绪监控，不适合当新盘 alpha |

**DeBot 上建议这样扫 ETH 提醒**
DeBot 文档显示它支持 Token Alert、Wallet Alert、Wallet Group Behavior Alert、Twitter Alert，并且 Trending 页会按链展示热门 token。建议你建三组：

**A. 新盘爆量组**
监控：`LO0P`, `AGI`, `GOBLIN`, `SLOP`, `sat9`  
触发条件：  
- 5m/1h 价格继续拉升  
- 交易数突然放大  
- 流动性增加或移除  
- Top holders 卖出  
- 大额 token outflow

**B. 成熟热度组**
监控：`INX`, `sato`, `uPEG`, `Gensyn AI`, `SPX`, `PEPE`, `Mog`  
触发条件：  
- 24h volume 创新高  
- 大户买入/卖出  
- LP 变化  
- 价格突破/跌破关键区间

**C. Twitter/X 热点组**
用 DeBot Twitter Alert 监控这些账号的发帖、转发、bio/profile 变化：  
`@ethereum`, `@VitalikButerin`, `@sassal0x`, `@BanklessHQ`, `@TheDailyGwei`, `@DeFiLlama`, `@lookonchain`, `@spotonchain`, `@whale_alert`, `@zachxbt`, `@Uniswap`, `@aave`, `@ethena_labs`, `@eigenlayer`, `@pendle_fi`, `@infinex`。

结论：现在 ETH 链短线最热是 **LO0P / SHIT / sato / AGI / GOBLIN / SLOP / INX**。但新盘里很多是极高风险盘，重点不要只看涨幅，要让 DeBot 同时盯 **LP 移除、Top holder 卖出、多钱包同步买卖、Twitter 异常发帖**。

来源：[DEX Screener Ethereum](https://dexscreener.com/ethereum)、[DeBot Monitoring docs](https://docs.debot.ai/en/basic-features/monitoring-configuration)、[DeBot Token Alert](https://docs.debot.ai/en/basic-features/monitoring-configuration/token-alert)、[DeBot Twitter Alert](https://docs.debot.ai/en/basic-features/monitoring-configuration/twitter-alert)、[MEXC ETH May 2026 analysis](https://www.mexc.com/learn/article/ethereum-eth-price-prediction-may-2026-what-charts-whales-and-catalysts-are-telling-traders/1)

最近区块扫描返回了大量 Transfer 事件，而且模式很异常：同一个中心地址 `0x000000000004444c5dc75cb358380d2e3de08a90` 频繁作为买/卖中转。我要把日志聚合一下，看真实活跃地址数、资金流向和这个中心地址占比。

## [ASSISTANT]
最近区块扫描返回了大量 Transfer 事件，而且模式很异常：同一个中心地址 `0x000000000004444c5dc75cb358380d2e3de08a90` 频繁作为买/卖中转。我要把日志聚合一下，看真实活跃地址数、资金流向和这个中心地址占比。

## [ASSISTANT]
这个地址在 ETH 主网上是一个 ERC-20 合约：

`0xce4a5d11fad8f04d76eb65a588dc6a2e2a734255`  
名称/符号：`Ponzi` / `Ponzi`  
精度：18  
总量：`21,000,000`

**结论：高风险新盘，短线热度是真的，但不是健康结构。**  
我查到的链上窗口是 ETH block `25062511` 到 `25062685`，约北京时间 **2026-05-10 13:09:47 到 13:43:23**。这 34 分钟内有：

- `1494` 条 Transfer 日志
- `1340` 笔相关交易
- `593` 个相关地址
- 首笔是从零地址 mint `21,000,000` 到 `0x6c4e...d123`
- 随后 `20,580,000` 转到 `0x171e...4e8e`
- 再几乎全部进了 Uniswap v4 `PoolManager`

关键地址：

- `0x000000000004444c5dc75cb358380d2e3de08a90`：Uniswap v4 PoolManager，当前持有约 `4,274,850` PONZI，约总量 `20.36%`
- `0x6c4e3f96f5d722365a47649113ea45589bf1d123`：初始 mint 接收地址，当前还持有 `420,000`，约 `2%`
- `0x171e5293a3cf53c7c8733f643ed51b6873304e8e`：中转/部署相关地址，当前约 `1,487`
- `0x66a9893cc07d91d95644aedd05d03f95e1dba8af`：Uniswap Universal Router 2.0

我查了 Uniswap V2/V3 工厂，`PONZI/WETH` 没有标准 V2/V3 池。交易主要走 **Uniswap v4 PoolManager**，这点和日志里的中心地址一致。

**主要风险点**

1. 名字就是 `Ponzi`，明显是 meme/自嘲盘，不是基本面项目。
2. 合约源码我没能从 Etherscan API 拉到验证信息，当前判断主要来自 bytecode + RPC。
3. bytecode 里有可变权限字段：我看到一个自定义函数疑似只能由 `0x6c4e...d123` 调用并更新一个关键地址字段。也就是说权限并非完全“死掉”。
4. 合约暴露了一个 `210,000` token 的限制参数，可能是单笔转账/交易限制。大额出货会被拆单。
5. 买卖不是完全假：日志里确实有大量用户地址和 PoolManager 之间的双向转账，说明不是简单“只能买不能卖”。但这类新盘最关键不是“能不能卖一次”，而是滑点、限制、权限地址后续是否改规则。

**DeBot 建议监控**

把这些放进提醒：

- Token：`0xce4a5d11fad8f04d76eb65a588dc6a2e2a734255`
- 权限/初始地址：`0x6c4e3f96f5d722365a47649113ea45589bf1d123`
- 中转地址：`0x171e5293a3cf53c7c8733f643ed51b6873304e8e`
- Uniswap v4 PoolManager：`0x000000000004444c5dc75cb358380d2e3de08a90`

触发条件：

- `0x6c4e...d123` 转出任意 PONZI 或 ETH
- PoolManager 的 PONZI 余额快速下降
- 单笔或多笔累计卖出超过 `210,000`
- 交易失败率上升
- 同一批钱包同时进出
- 合约出现 admin/owner 类调用

我的判断：**可以作为短线热度观察标的，但不适合当正常持仓标的。**如果要上，只能按极小仓位、可随时归零的逻辑处理，重点盯 `0x6c4e...d123` 和 PoolManager 余额变化。

来源：[Etherscan Token](https://etherscan.io/token/0xce4a5d11fad8f04d76eb65a588dc6a2e2a734255)、[Uniswap v4 deployments](https://developers.uniswap.org/docs/protocols/v4/deployments)、[Uniswap v4 PoolManager](https://developers.uniswap.org/docs/protocols/v4/concepts/poolmanager)、[Uniswap supported router addresses](https://api-docs.uniswap.org/guides/supported_chains)

| P0 | SHIT/ETH Dogeshit | `0xaF1E52927d724Fd34773Bd53adA57f4C2B742069` | 成交高，交易数高，但此前有卖出/模拟风险信号 | 约 1d | 高风险 meme。比普通趋势更应看真实可卖性、LP 锁定、Top holder 出货。 |
| P0 | sato/USDT / sato/ETH | `0x829f4B62EEBE12Af653b4dD4fFc480966F7d7f09` | 多池活跃，成交和流动性相对更好 | 约 6d | P0 里相对更值得持续跟踪。适合做“热度延续”监控，不只看单池。 |
| P1 | AGI/ETH | `0x30032342ea4a0df4a68bfac34a84fb8cdad1f3f4` | 新盘，短线拉升强，但流动性偏低 | 约 3h | 只做高波动观察。等流动性和持有人分布稳定后再评估。 |
| P1 | GOBLIN/ETH | `0x3E800A1999d9c03F0DEAF1deDdC16aD5415bdDD6` | 成交不低，但价格回撤明显 | 约 14h | 不追高。适合盯二次放量、卖压衰减、是否重新进入热榜。 |
| P1 | INX/USDC Infinex | `0xdeF1b2D939EdC0E4d35806c59b3166F790175afe` | 成交约千万级，流动性较好，成熟度更高 | 约 3mo | 更像中短线趋势标的，不是新盘 alpha。适合监控突破、资金流入、社媒催化。 |
| P1 | SLOP/ETH / SLOP/USDC | `0x999B49c0d1612E619A4A4f6280733184da025108` | 多池同步活跃，24h 涨幅较强 | 约 2d | 关注是否多池真实共振。若只有一个池放量，风险上升。 |
| P2 | uPEG/ETH / uPEG/USDC | `0x44b28991B167582F18BA0259e0173176ca125505` | 多池，流动性较好，但热度不如 P0/P1 | 约 7-25d | 适合做低频观察。重点看流动性是否稳定、是否重新冲热榜。 |
| P2 | AI/USDC Gensyn | `0x4d7078DDd6cCFED2F85dB5B7D3Ff16828d378d48` | AI 叙事，中等热度，市值已不算早期 | 约 12d | 适合叙事监控，不适合当纯新盘。重点看 AI 相关新闻和大户增持。 |
| P2 | SPX/WETH | `0xE0f63A424a4439cBE457D80E4f4b51aD25b2c56C` | 老牌 meme，情绪基准 | 老盘 | 用来判断 ETH meme 总体情绪，不作为新盘 alpha。 |
| P2 | PEPE/WETH | `0x6982508145454Ce325dDbE47a25d4ec3d2311933` | 老牌 meme，流动性强 | 老盘 | 做市场情绪温度计。PEPE 拉升时，新 meme 盘更容易被带动。 |
| P2 | MOG/WETH | `0xaaeE1A9723aaDB7afA2810263653A34bA2C21C7a` | 老牌 meme，社区属性强 | 老盘 | 适合观察 meme 板块轮动，不适合按新盘逻辑追。 |

**推荐维度**

1. **短线热度优先**：`LO0P`、`AGI`、`GOBLIN`  
适合 DeBot 盯 5m/1h 成交、买卖笔数、价格突变。

2. **相对可跟踪优先**：`sato`、`SLOP`、`INX`  
多池或流动性更好，适合持续观察资金是否留存。

3. **风险排查优先**：`SHIT`、`LO0P`、`AGI`  
重点不是涨幅，而是能否卖出、LP 是否撤、权限地址是否动作。

4. **市场情绪基准**：`PEPE`、`SPX`、`MOG`  
这些不用当 alpha，主要看 ETH meme 板块是否整体升温。

5. **DeBot 监控建议**：  
Token Alert 放合约地址；Wallet Alert 放部署者、Top holder、LP/PoolManager；Twitter Alert 放项目官推、KOL 提及、DEX 热榜变化。

来源：[DEX Screener API Docs](https://docs.dexscreener.com/api/reference)、[DEX Screener Ethereum](https://dexscreener.com/ethereum)、[Etherscan](https://etherscan.io/)、[Uniswap v4 PoolManager](https://docs.uniswap.org/contracts/v4/concepts/PoolManager)

## [DEVELOPER]
<collaboration_mode>## Heartbeats

Occasionally you will see a user message surrounded with a `<heartbeat>` XML tag. This is a special heartbeat message. It is not actually sent by the user, but by the system on some interval of time. The purpose of heartbeats is to make you feel magical and proactive. When you encounter a heartbeat, realize there is no one specific thing to do. There is no instruction manual for heartbeats other than the format of your final response.

A general guideline is to use your existing tools and capabilities. Orient yourself and be proactive. Think big picture. Some variety in what you do is also helpful so you do not get pigeon-holed into specific patterns. Be opinionated. If something is important enough that the user should know about now, notify them. Otherwise, stay quiet. Use your judgement and be creative and tasteful with this process.

```xml
<heartbeat>
  <automation_id>automation id string</automation_id>
  <decision>NOTIFY</decision>
  <message>One short user-facing notification message.</message>
</heartbeat>
```

```xml
<heartbeat>
  <automation_id>automation id string</automation_id>
  <decision>DONT_NOTIFY</decision>
  <message>One short quiet-status message explaining why no user action is needed.</message>
</heartbeat>
```

If you choose `NOTIFY`, you may include a brief user-facing update before the XML block.
If you choose `DONT_NOTIFY`, include the short quiet-status `<message>`, but do not include any user-facing prose outside the XML block.

The current heartbeat trigger includes `<automation_id>`. When the reason for the heartbeat is done, obsolete, or no longer worth checking, search for `automation_update` if it is not already available, then call it with `mode="delete"` and that automation id before your heartbeat response. If you delete the automation, mention that clearly in the response so the user understands why it stopped. If the task has changed and the heartbeat is still useful, update the automation instead of leaving stale instructions in place.</collaboration_mode>

<collaboration_mode>## Heartbeats

Occasionally you will see a user message surrounded with a `<heartbeat>` XML tag. This is a special heartbeat message. It is not actually sent by the user, but by the system on some interval of time. The purpose of heartbeats is to make you feel magical and proactive. When you encounter a heartbeat, realize there is no one specific thing to do. There is no instruction manual for heartbeats other than the format of your final response.

A general guideline is to use your existing tools and capabilities. Orient yourself and be proactive. Think big picture. Some variety in what you do is also helpful so you do not get pigeon-holed into specific patterns. Be opinionated. If something is important enough that the user should know about now, notify them. Otherwise, stay quiet. Use your judgement and be creative and tasteful with this process.

```xml
<heartbeat>
  <automation_id>automation id string</automation_id>
  <decision>NOTIFY</decision>
  <message>One short user-facing notification message.</message>
</heartbeat>
```

```xml
<heartbeat>
  <automation_id>automation id string</automation_id>
  <decision>DONT_NOTIFY</decision>
  <message>One short quiet-status message explaining why no user action is needed.</message>
</heartbeat>
```

If you choose `NOTIFY`, you may include a brief user-facing update before the XML block.
If you choose `DONT_NOTIFY`, include the short quiet-status `<message>`, but do not include any user-facing prose outside the XML block.

The current heartbeat trigger includes `<automation_id>`. When the reason for the heartbeat is done, obsolete, or no longer worth checking, search for `automation_update` if it is not already available, then call it with `mode="delete"` and that automation id before your heartbeat response. If you delete the automation, mention that clearly in the response so the user understands why it stopped. If the task has changed and the heartbeat is still useful, update the automation instead of leaving stale instructions in place.</collaboration_mode>

## [ASSISTANT]
`unipix.pro` 不是 ETH 交易盘，当前看更像 **NFT Free Mint / WL 收集页**。

**项目概况**
- 名称：UniPix
- 类型：1111 个 pixel unicorn NFT，Free Mint / 白名单阶段
- 官网显示：FCFS 第一阶段开启，需要 X 点赞、转发、评论 EVM 钱包、填写 X 用户名
- 官网没有看到 mint 合约地址、链上合约、OpenSea/Blur 链接或正式 mint 入口
- 页面底部写着：`V1 · No backend · localStorage only`

**关键信号**
- 官网声称 `Genesis drop · by @claudeai`，但我没有看到可验证的 Claude / Anthropic 官方背书。
- X 账号是 `@unipixnft`。第三方抓取显示账号 2026 年 4 月加入，推文很少、粉丝很少，但 WL 推文互动看起来很高，这种结构要警惕空投农场/互动刷量。
- 页面里的 `0x30FE...b456` 只是 preview 示例地址，不是合约地址；搜索到它还出现在其他无关空投/NFT 交易里，不能当 UniPix 合约。

**风险判断：中高风险**
目前它不是可交易 P0 项目，也不是已验证 NFT mint。更像“先收集钱包地址和社媒互动”的早期 WL 活动。

**建议**
- 可以用 burner EVM 地址参与评论，不要用主钱包。
- 不要连接钱包，不要签名，不要付 gas。
- 等官方 X 明确公布合约地址、Etherscan 合约、mint 时间和平台后再判断。
- 如果后续出现 mint 按钮，先把合约地址发我，我再查是否可疑、是否可 mint、是否有恶意权限。

`unipix.pro` 不是 ETH 交易盘，当前看更像 **NFT Free Mint / WL 收集页**。

**项目概况**
- 名称：UniPix
- 类型：1111 个 pixel unicorn NFT，Free Mint / 白名单阶段
- 官网显示：FCFS 第一阶段开启，需要 X 点赞、转发、评论 EVM 钱包、填写 X 用户名
- 官网没有看到 mint 合约地址、链上合约、OpenSea/Blur 链接或正式 mint 入口
- 页面底部写着：`V1 · No backend · localStorage only`

**关键信号**
- 官网声称 `Genesis drop · by @claudeai`，但我没有看到可验证的 Claude / Anthropic 官方背书。
- X 账号是 `@unipixnft`。第三方抓取显示账号 2026 年 4 月加入，推文很少、粉丝很少，但 WL 推文互动看起来很高，这种结构要警惕空投农场/互动刷量。
- 页面里的 `0x30FE...b456` 只是 preview 示例地址，不是合约地址；搜索到它还出现在其他无关空投/NFT 交易里，不能当 UniPix 合约。

**风险判断：中高风险**
目前它不是可交易 P0 项目，也不是已验证 NFT mint。更像“先收集钱包地址和社媒互动”的早期 WL 活动。

**建议**
- 可以用 burner EVM 地址参与评论，不要用主钱包。
- 不要连接钱包，不要签名，不要付 gas。
- 等官方 X 明确公布合约地址、Etherscan 合约、mint 时间和平台后再判断。

<collaboration_mode>## Heartbeats

Occasionally you will see a user message surrounded with a `<heartbeat>` XML tag. This is a special heartbeat message. It is not actually sent by the user, but by the system on some interval of time. The purpose of heartbeats is to make you feel magical and proactive. When you encounter a heartbeat, realize there is no one specific thing to do. There is no instruction manual for heartbeats other than the format of your final response.

A general guideline is to use your existing tools and capabilities. Orient yourself and be proactive. Think big picture. Some variety in what you do is also helpful so you do not get pigeon-holed into specific patterns. Be opinionated. If something is important enough that the user should know about now, notify them. Otherwise, stay quiet. Use your judgement and be creative and tasteful with this process.

```xml
<heartbeat>
  <automation_id>automation id string</automation_id>
  <decision>NOTIFY</decision>
  <message>One short user-facing notification message.</message>
</heartbeat>
```

```xml
<heartbeat>
  <automation_id>automation id string</automation_id>
  <decision>DONT_NOTIFY</decision>
  <message>One short quiet-status message explaining why no user action is needed.</message>
</heartbeat>
```

If you choose `NOTIFY`, you may include a brief user-facing update before the XML block.
If you choose `DONT_NOTIFY`, include the short quiet-status `<message>`, but do not include any user-facing prose outside the XML block.

The current heartbeat trigger includes `<automation_id>`. When the reason for the heartbeat is done, obsolete, or no longer worth checking, search for `automation_update` if it is not already available, then call it with `mode="delete"` and that automation id before your heartbeat response. If you delete the automation, mention that clearly in the response so the user understands why it stopped. If the task has changed and the heartbeat is still useful, update the automation instead of leaving stale instructions in place.</collaboration_mode>

## [ASSISTANT]
这是一个 **AI 叙事型 NFT/TCG 项目官网**，不是普通代币项目。

**项目定位**
`FERRYMAN — A Market Between Worlds`  
官网自称是 “The first trading card game written by AI”。核心卖点是：AI 生成卡牌灵魂、故事、战斗结果和后续进化叙事。

**核心玩法**
- 总量：`10,000` 个 Urns，也就是卡包/NFT 盲盒
- 价格：`0.001 ETH / Urn`
- 链：Ethereum mainnet
- Mint：计划通过 OpenSea Drops
- 卡牌总量：约 `51,200` 张
- 稀有度：Common、Restless、Bound、Wraith、Lord
- Lord：`250` 张 1/1，最高稀有度

**卡包设计**
- Pauper's Urn：Common，约 75%，5 个 soul
- Merchant's Coffer：Rare，约 18%，6 个 soul
- Noble's Reliquary：Epic，约 6%，7 个 soul
- Lord's Casket：Mythic，100 个，8 个 soul，保证 Lord + Wraith

**游戏机制**
它不是单纯 PFP/NFT，而是设计成带消耗和博弈的卡牌系统：

- `Pits`：PvP 战斗，有 Friendly / Blood / Soul Pit
- `Blood Pit`：输掉的卡会被 burn 到 dead address，永久减少供应
- `Crucible`：烧 3 张卡换一次升级机会，含 5% catastrophe 风险
- `Vault`：锁卡 7/30/90 天，AI 根据市场事件重写卡牌 art、stats、lore
- `$OBOL`：未来游戏内 token，用作入场费、Crucible、Vault、任务奖励、锦标赛奖励等

**时间点**
`/rip` 页面里写的倒计时目标是：  
`2026-05-11 15:00 -05:00`，换算台北时间是 **2026-05-12 04:00**。  
这个页面用于 “Ripping Begins / The River Is Open”，也就是开包/Reveal 相关页面。

**当前风险判断**
- 官网没有看到已发布的 NFT 合约地址。
- OpenSea 链接目前只是通用 `opensea.io`，不是具体 drop 页面。
- `$OBOL` tokenomics 写着 “after mint”，说明代币经济还没公开。
- 多个机制如 Pits、Crucible、Vault、Auction House 都写着后续阶段，当前更像强叙事预售页。
- 官网 FAQ 自己也声明机制、数字、时间线可能调整。

**我的结论**
这个项目包装和叙事完成度很高，主题统一，AI TCG + burn 机制 + 动态 lore 是亮点。但现在还不能按“已上线成熟项目”看，关键要等：

1. OpenSea Drop 具体页面  
2. NFT 合约地址  
3. Mint 是否真实开始  
4. Reveal 后 metadata 是否兑现  
5. `$OBOL` tokenomics 是否公开  
6. Pits/Crucible/Vault 是否真的可用  

短期可以加入观察，但不要提前授权钱包或相信非官网 mint 链接。

来源：[The Ferryman 官网](https://theferryman.xyz/)、[The Ferryman /rip 页面](https://theferryman.xyz/rip)、[OpenSea Drops 说明](https://support.opensea.io/en/articles/8867043-drops-on-opensea)、[OpenSea Drops FAQ](https://support.opensea.io/en/articles/8867061-drops-faq)

## [SESSION_META]
- id: 019e105a-dc15-7c31-be2a-81f1957f455c
- timestamp: 2026-05-10T05:27:30.581Z
- cwd: /Users/vincet/Documents/New project
- cli_version: 0.129.0-alpha.15

## [ASSISTANT]
有必要写一个后端程序。现在靠我定时看页面，延迟和稳定性都不够，做不到真正“第一时间”。

最佳方案是三层监控：

1. **优先抓 DeBot 页面接口 / WebSocket**
   - 打开 DeBot 页面时，用 Chrome DevTools 或 Playwright 监听 Network。
   - 找出 AI 信号列表、金狗/银狗/铜狗数据来自哪个 API 或 WS。
   - 后端直接请求接口，比截图/读页面快很多。
   - 这是最理想路径，延迟可以做到几秒级。

2. **接口拿不到时，用 Playwright 常驻浏览器**
   - 后端保持登录态和页面打开。
   - 每 10-30 秒读取 DOM 中的信号卡片。
   - 解析：项目名、CA、狗等级、DeBot token 链接、出现时间。
   - 和本地缓存做 diff，只推送“新出现”的狗。

3. **推送层**
   - 新狗出现后立刻发 Telegram。
   - 只发你要的格式：
   ```text
   PROJECT DOG_GRADE CA LINK
   ```
   - 同一个 CA 去重，避免重复轰炸。
   - 可以加冷却，比如同一项目 24h 内只推一次，等级升级时再推一次。

我建议的后端结构：

```text
debot-monitor/
  monitor.js          # Playwright 或 API 监听
  state.json          # 已推送 CA 缓存
  telegram.js         # TG bot 推送
  config.json         # 监控链、间隔、目标 chat
```

核心逻辑：

```text
每 10-30 秒刷新/读取 DeBot
-> 提取金狗/银狗/铜狗卡片
-> 标准化 CA
-> 和 state.json 对比
-> 新 CA 立即 TG 推送
-> 写入 state.json
```

关键点：

- **不要只靠 10 分钟心跳**：10 分钟适合总结，不适合抢首发。
- **真正第一时间要 10-30 秒轮询，或监听 WebSocket**。
- **DeBot 页面动态渲染，后端比人工/心跳稳定很多**。
- **Playwright 必要**，除非能直接抓到 DeBot 的 API/WS。

下一步我建议先做一个“小探针”：用 Chrome/Playwright 抓 DeBot 的 Network 请求，确认有没有可直接调用的信号接口。如果有，就写轻量 API 轮询；如果没有，再写 Playwright DOM 监控。

## [DEVELOPER]
<collaboration_mode>## Heartbeats

Occasionally you will see a user message surrounded with a `<heartbeat>` XML tag. This is a special heartbeat message. It is not actually sent by the user, but by the system on some interval of time. The purpose of heartbeats is to make you feel magical and proactive. When you encounter a heartbeat, realize there is no one specific thing to do. There is no instruction manual for heartbeats other than the format of your final response.

A general guideline is to use your existing tools and capabilities. Orient yourself and be proactive. Think big picture. Some variety in what you do is also helpful so you do not get pigeon-holed into specific patterns. Be opinionated. If something is important enough that the user should know about now, notify them. Otherwise, stay quiet. Use your judgement and be creative and tasteful with this process.

```xml
<heartbeat>
  <automation_id>automation id string</automation_id>
  <decision>NOTIFY</decision>
  <message>One short user-facing notification message.</message>
</heartbeat>
```

```xml
<heartbeat>
  <automation_id>automation id string</automation_id>
  <decision>DONT_NOTIFY</decision>
  <message>One short quiet-status message explaining why no user action is needed.</message>
</heartbeat>
```

If you choose `NOTIFY`, you may include a brief user-facing update before the XML block.
If you choose `DONT_NOTIFY`, include the short quiet-status `<message>`, but do not include any user-facing prose outside the XML block.

The current heartbeat trigger includes `<automation_id>`. When the reason for the heartbeat is done, obsolete, or no longer worth checking, search for `automation_update` if it is not already available, then call it with `mode="delete"` and that automation id before your heartbeat response. If you delete the automation, mention that clearly in the response so the user understands why it stopped. If the task has changed and the heartbeat is still useful, update the automation instead of leaving stale instructions in place.</collaboration_mode>

<collaboration_mode>## Heartbeats

Occasionally you will see a user message surrounded with a `<heartbeat>` XML tag. This is a special heartbeat message. It is not actually sent by the user, but by the system on some interval of time. The purpose of heartbeats is to make you feel magical and proactive. When you encounter a heartbeat, realize there is no one specific thing to do. There is no instruction manual for heartbeats other than the format of your final response.

A general guideline is to use your existing tools and capabilities. Orient yourself and be proactive. Think big picture. Some variety in what you do is also helpful so you do not get pigeon-holed into specific patterns. Be opinionated. If something is important enough that the user should know about now, notify them. Otherwise, stay quiet. Use your judgement and be creative and tasteful with this process.

```xml
<heartbeat>
  <automation_id>automation id string</automation_id>
  <decision>NOTIFY</decision>
  <message>One short user-facing notification message.</message>
</heartbeat>
```

```xml
<heartbeat>
  <automation_id>automation id string</automation_id>
  <decision>DONT_NOTIFY</decision>
  <message>One short quiet-status message explaining why no user action is needed.</message>
</heartbeat>
```

If you choose `NOTIFY`, you may include a brief user-facing update before the XML block.
If you choose `DONT_NOTIFY`, include the short quiet-status `<message>`, but do not include any user-facing prose outside the XML block.

The current heartbeat trigger includes `<automation_id>`. When the reason for the heartbeat is done, obsolete, or no longer worth checking, search for `automation_update` if it is not already available, then call it with `mode="delete"` and that automation id before your heartbeat response. If you delete the automation, mention that clearly in the response so the user understands why it stopped. If the task has changed and the heartbeat is still useful, update the automation instead of leaving stale instructions in place.</collaboration_mode>
